28	**Async fetch + egress hardening (server v0.4.0, 2026-07-01):** `fetch` is now NON-BLOCKING — it enqueues onto a single background worker runspace and returns `{ job_id, status:"queued" }` immediately; poll the NEW `fetch_status` tool (job_id → queued|running|done|failed + result map, or all jobs newest-first; ~30min TTL eviction). Tools now 7 (added fetch_status). Motivation: a cold/large `export.arxiv.org/e-print` fetch could outlast the MCP client's call timeout even when arXiv was healthy — verified NOT proxy/IPv6/TLS (arXiv is IPv4-only Fastly, no proxy; direct HttpClient pull was fast). Root cause was the timeout budget: `-TimeoutSec` in pwsh 7.6 is only an alias for `-ConnectionTimeoutSeconds` (total), and the code never set `-OperationTimeoutSeconds` (per-read IDLE timeout), so a stalled socket hung the full 180s×3-retry budget. Fixes in `src/arxiv.ps1`: both Invoke-WebRequest sites now set `-OperationTimeoutSeconds` (idle) + `-HttpVersion 1.1` + a smaller `-ConnectionTimeoutSeconds`. Concurrency design: ONE worker (not a pool) so all downloads stay behind arXiv's 3s floor; the rate clock moved from a script var to a synchronized cross-runspace holder (`$script:ArxivRateState` + `Use-ArxivRateState`) so main-runspace search/metadata and worker-runspace fetch share one floor. Worker = raw `RunspaceFactory` (NOT Start-ThreadJob — its module isn't on the portable path). `fetch_status` also takes an OPT-IN long-poll `wait=N` (server-capped ~55s in `Get-ArxivFetchJob -WaitSeconds`, single client per stdio server so blocking the main loop is fine) that returns the instant a job goes terminal — for the "ready to collect, wait like a test run" moment, not the move-on phase. Worker-liveness now DETECTED (`Test-ArxivWorkerAlive` = InvocationStateInfo Running/NotStarted): a long-poll bails immediately on a dead worker and the view is flagged `stalled=true`+`worker_error`. `Clear-ArxivStaleJobs` uses tolerant `[datetime]::TryParse` (a malformed finished_at must never crash a status poll). Verified: Pester (38 offline, incl. job-registry + long-poll blocks), direct dot-source e2e (incl. single blocking wait→done), live JSON-RPC round-trip. Download COMPLETENESS now guarded (`Invoke-ArxivFetch`): bytes==Content-Length (conclusive, TLS guarantees bytes) or — when Content-Length withheld — a source gzip must inflate AND its size match the ISIZE trailer (`Test-ArxivGzipIntact`; .NET GZipStream does NOT reliably throw on a truncated deflate stream, so count-vs-ISIZE is what catches it). Fixes the "incomplete response body" failure the reference arxiv-mcp-server documented for larger export.arxiv.org e-prints; truncated/incomplete now class transient→retry. ROOT CAUSE of the PDF-fine-but-source-times-out asymmetry: PDF = hot edge-cached static CDN artifact (arxiv.org/pdf); e-print source = dynamically-packaged, rarely-cached (origin), throttled, size-variable → higher/variable TTFB + truncation risk (host export-vs-arxiv.org is SECONDARY, cache warmth dominates — verified both serve e-print w/ Content-Length). Reference server does HTML-first→PDF-fallback (pymupdf4llm), NEVER fetches source — source-fetching is our own addition. A lower-level SocketsHttpHandler fetch engine (connection reuse, Range/resume for big source, byte-rate watchdog; the .NET analog of the reference's httpx connect/read split) is an EVIDENCE-GATED brief at `issues/arxiv-async/sockets-httphandler-fetch-engine.md` (curl/wget/BITS weighed + declined; IWR already at parity via -ConnectionTimeoutSeconds/-OperationTimeoutSeconds). LIMITATION: in-flight jobs lost on server restart (disk `.part`/sidecar is source of truth). TRUE auto-wake (fire→harness re-invokes agent on completion, WITHOUT a subagent — via a thin harness-tracked background *process* watching a completion marker the worker writes; background process ≠ subagent on every axis) is DESIGNED-NOT-BUILT in `issues/arxiv-async/autowake-background-process.md`. See [[powershell-text-mutation-traps]] for the pwsh-quirk lineage.

1	# Procurement
2	
3	`procurement` is the reusable Python capability layer for scholarly discovery, acquisition, and source
4	preparation. It has no MCP dependency in its implementation graph; the MCP SDK is consumed only by
5	`src/mcp-servers/procurement_mcp`.
6	
7	The package is divided by responsibility:
8	
9	```text
10	application.py       owned runtime dependencies and lifecycle
11	composition.py       configuration-to-application assembly
12	configuration/       validated configuration models and data loading
13	domain/              immutable work, discovery, provider, metadata, deposit, and operation contracts
14	operations/          discovery, metadata, acquisition, materialization, and catalog use cases
15	providers/           provider declarations and adapters
16	source/              source contracts, extraction, tree identity, LaTeX inspection, and findings
17	storage/             acquisition/deposit transactions, catalog roots, and schema-backed persistence
18	transport/           HTTP policy and transport primitives
19	```
20	
21	The former flat `archive`, `http`, `models`, `payloads`, `settings`, `source`, `staging`, and `services`
22	modules have no compatibility aliases. Shared model infrastructure, portable deposit validation,
23	scholarly works, discovery envelopes, provider descriptors, metadata evidence, and acquisition contracts
24	live in focused `domain` modules. Acquisition planning and safe-route policy are separate from durable
25	receipt and operation-result contracts.
26	
27	The current discovery plane contains OpenAlex, Semantic Scholar, arXiv, and Zenodo adapters behind an
28	explicit provider catalog. Each adapter owns one immutable descriptor containing its category, roles,
29	capabilities, and honored search constraints. `DiscoveryService` provides federated search, graph traversal, reference
30	resolution, and provider lookup. Provider-specific search constraints are declared; federated search emits
31	an explicit error report instead of silently weakening a constraint. `WorkRecord` preserves every provider
32	identity participating in a merge.
33	
34	`MetadataService` independently prepares `codex-scientiae/deposit-metadata/0.1` bundles for article
35	publication. It can resolve from the acquisition artifact identity or from a caller-selected DOI that is
36	recorded separately from byte provenance. arXiv and Zenodo are artifact origins and metadata authorities; Sci-Hub is an artifact-access
37	source; OpenAlex and Semantic Scholar are metadata aggregators. Aggregator metadata is accepted only after
38	identity matching and never replaces artifact provenance. Each bundle carries the exact selected API body,
39	its digest, and the normalized `article.json` projection. “Exact” means the HTTP-decoded entity payload
40	consumed by the provider normalizer, not compressed on-wire framing.
41	
42	`article.json` is the invariant output rather than the definition of one workflow. Provider acquisition,
43	configured local import, LaTeX inspection, and API metadata resolution are separable evidence producers;
44	the article publisher validates their combination against a versioned article profile. The current profile
45	is LaTeX-backed. A future PDF profile will provide a second path into the same sentinel contract.
46	
47	```python
48	import asyncio
49	
50	from procurement.composition import build_application
51	from procurement.domain.discovery import SearchRequest
52	
53	
54	async def main() -> None:
55	    async with build_application() as application:
56	        result = await application.discovery.search(
57	            SearchRequest(query="persistent homology", limit=10),
58	            source="all",
59	        )
60	        print(result.model_dump(mode="json"))
61	
62	
63	asyncio.run(main())
64	```
65	
66	Provider endpoints, request floors, timeouts, attempts, and default sources live in the version 2
67	`configs/defaults.json`. That data also names the confined staging root, logical local-import inboxes, source catalogs, and payload and
68	archive limits. `CODEX_SCIENTIAE_ROOT` can select the workspace explicitly. `CODEX_SCHOLAR_MAILTO` supplies a contact address and
69	`OPENALEX_API_KEY` and `SEMANTIC_SCHOLAR_API_KEY` supply optional provider credentials.
70	Provider categories and capabilities are adapter declarations rather than configuration data.
71	`providers/builtin.py` is the built-in construction catalog; each entry binds an adapter descriptor,
72	constructor shape, and any provider-specific config validation. Composition accepts an extended immutable
73	factory catalog, so adding another adapter does not require a provider switch in `composition.py`.
74	The runtime catalog distinguishes metadata aggregators, scholarly repositories, and access-only sources;
75	Sci-Hub is classified as access-only rather than as a repository or metadata authority.
76	
77	Filesystem trust boundary: composition captures and retains the physical identities of the staging root,
78	local-import inboxes, and article catalogs for the application lifetime. Acquisition transactions pin each
79	item below the retained staging root; HTTP and local-import byte transfer, hashing, recovery, receipt access,
80	and publication are relative to those retained generations. Local import also reads through the retained
81	inbox descriptor. A replacement route is either blocked or causes the operation to fail without writing to
82	or reporting success against the replacement. Source materialization retains the acquisition item, catalog,
83	document, private extraction tree, and final source tree for their complete transaction intervals. Archive
84	expansion, source inspection, archive/PDF copies, metadata publication, tree installation, and
85	`article.json` publication use those retained generations. Inventory rebuild separately pins its catalog
86	generation across sentinel reads and publication.
87	
88	`AcquisitionService` asks an artifact-capable provider for an immutable internal plan, then streams each
89	requested form through one shared transaction. Plans never cross the MCP execution boundary. Downloads are
90	bounded, redirect-confined, content-checked, locally SHA-256 measured, and checked against provider-native
91	integrity evidence when present. Non-loopback routes require HTTPS and cannot redirect to plaintext HTTP.
92	Successful forms are monotonically collated into schema-validated `acquisition.json` within the pinned item
93	generation. Lock, validation, hashing, and publication work runs outside the MCP event loop. That
94	receipt makes no unpacked or source-ready claim; `article.json` remains the canonical sentinel, currently
95	published by the validated LaTeX-source profile.
96	Background jobs remain deferred until the synchronous operations have a separate lifecycle contract.
97	
98	`LocalImportService` is the parallel custody route for files already downloaded by a human or another tool.
99	Clients name a configured inbox, a portable direct-child leaf, and a deposit slug; they cannot submit a host
100	path or URL. The service sniffs and validates gzip source or PDF bytes, makes an independent staged copy, and
101	publishes the same `acquisition.json` contract with `local-import` custody. It does not claim that a DOI or
102	arXiv identity proves the origin of those bytes.
103	
104	`SourceMaterializationService` consumes an existing `acquisition.json`; it never downloads an artifact.
105	It requires exactly one receipted source form, copies staged bytes into an independent inode, safely expands
106	gzip-wrapped tar or single-TeX source, validates the complete LaTeX closure, publishes the canonical
107	`{slug}.tar.gz` and `{slug}-tex/` forms without replacement, and calls `jsonl_engine` to publish
108	`article.json` last. Bibliography is selected by one typed strategy: acquisition artifact identity,
109	caller-supplied DOI, or deliberate omission. Explicit DOI resolution is identity-checked against API results
110	and any DOI declared by the validated LaTeX closure. There is no best-effort mode
111	that could permanently mint an accidentally metadata-free sentinel. Tar input requires a canonical
112	terminator and zero-only padding. The first sentinel freezes PDF inclusion, so a later receipt cannot mutate
113	an existing article or leave an orphan PDF. The fixed seven-probe ledger remains independent evidence of
114	source integrity.
115	
116	The materialization transaction publishes `article.json` last. It uses no separate source journal because
117	every persistent pre-sentinel component is immutable and independently validated: exact archive, PDF,
118	metadata, and source-tree state is adopted on retry, while a conflict fails without replacement. Exact
119	PID-plus-process-serial private tree scratch is the only mutable intermediate and is swept recursively under
120	the generation-keyed source lease. An interrupted retry therefore preserves established bytes and mtimes
121	and does not repeat a metadata request whose validated bundle already exists.
122	
123	`ArticleCatalogService` resolves configured catalog names, inspects safe direct-child `article.json`
124	sentinels, and independently rebuilds `inventory.jsonl`. Rebuild pins one physical catalog generation from
125	sentinel reads through main and sidecar publication. Default rebuilds publish with atomic no-replace; only
126	`force=True` replaces an existing inventory in that pinned generation. It does not acquire or prepare
127	sources. The three operations therefore compose without becoming one transaction:
128	
129	```text
130	acquire one item         -> acquisition.json
131	or import one local item -> acquisition.json
132	materialize one source  -> article.json
133	rebuild one catalog     -> inventory.jsonl
134	```
135	
136	A PDF can currently be imported, receipted, and used as the human basis for an explicit DOI lookup. A lone
137	PDF does not yet mint `article.json`: the current article contract requires a validated LaTeX archive and
138	tree. PDF-only publication requires its own versioned source-form validation profile so it cannot weaken the
139	existing sentinel by exception.
140

```
1	# Procurement MCP
2	
3	`scientiae-procurement` is the Python MCP projection of the procurement discovery and metadata services. It
4	owns SDK registration, tool schemas, prompts, and application lifespan. It does not own provider algorithms
5	or cross-provider workflows. Provider arguments are names validated by the backend catalog; protocol
6	schemas do not duplicate a fixed provider enum. `list_procurement_providers` reports the current category,
7	roles, capabilities, and honored search constraints.
8	
9	`server.py` is the composition and lifespan owner. Protocol-only request aliases and response projections
10	live in `contracts.py`, shared request context and cancellation handling live in `runtime.py`, and concrete
11	handlers are registered in protocol order from the modules under `tools/`. Tool modules resolve application
12	services only through the active MCP request context.
13	
14	Run the stdio server from the repository environment:
15	
16	```pwsh
17	.venv/Scripts/python.exe -m procurement_mcp
18	```
19	
20	The installed script name is `scientiae-procurement`. Discovery uses `discover_search`,
21	`discover_related`, `resolve_reference`, and `get_work`. `list_procurement_providers` exposes the distinct
22	artifact and metadata roles. `prepare_source_deposit_metadata` returns a validated, self-contained metadata
23	bundle with the exact HTTP-decoded entity payload consumed by its provider normalizer, a digest, and
24	identity-checked fallback history. It does not claim to preserve compressed on-wire framing.
25	`prepare_article_metadata_by_doi` is the independent route for a caller-selected DOI. It reads an existing
26	acquisition receipt so its identity anchor stays separate from, but correctly bound to, byte provenance; it
27	does not claim that the bytes came from the selected metadata provider.
28	
29	Artifact retrieval is deliberately separate. `plan_artifact_acquisition` returns a URL-free summary from a
30	fresh server-side provider plan; `acquire_artifact` replans internally, downloads requested source/PDF/HTML
31	forms to configured staging, and publishes or validates `acquisition.json`.
32	`get_acquisition_receipt` revalidates a staged receipt and every file it names. None of these tools accepts
33	a client-selected URL, serialized plan, absolute destination, or arbitrary storage root.
34	`list_local_import_inboxes` exposes configured logical inbox names, and `import_local_artifact` validates a
35	portable direct-child PDF or gzip source before publishing the same acquisition receipt with explicit
36	`local-import` custody. It accepts neither a host path nor a URL.
37	
38	The application retains the physical identities of every configured storage root. Acquisition and local
39	import use the retained staging, item, and inbox generations for byte transfer, validation, recovery, and
40	receipt publication. Replacement is either blocked or makes the operation fail without writing to or
41	reporting success against the replacement. Source materialization retains the acquisition item, catalog,
42	document, private extraction tree, and final tree through archive expansion, form and metadata publication,
43	and article publication. Inventory rebuild pins its selected catalog generation through publication.
44	
45	`list_article_catalogs` exposes the configured names accepted by the filesystem operations; clients cannot
46	submit a root path. `materialize_source_deposit` consumes one existing acquisition receipt, safely unpacks
47	and validates its source, optionally copies its receipted PDF, and publishes `article.json` last. Its typed
48	metadata strategy is `artifact-identity`, `explicit-doi`, or `omit`; the first two reuse or persist
49	`{slug}.api-metadata.json`, while omit never calls a metadata provider. An explicit DOI is cross-checked
50	against any DOI declared by the validated LaTeX closure. It does not acquire bytes or
51	rebuild an inventory.
52	
53	`inspect_article_catalog` reports the current direct-child source-ready population without writing.
54	`rebuild_article_inventory` independently rebuilds `inventory.jsonl` from that population and requires
55	`force=true` to replace an existing inventory. `prepare_source_deposit_metadata` remains useful as the
56	independent metadata-only route. API metadata supplies bibliographic evidence; source materialization
57	separately supplies the seven source-integrity findings.
58	
59	The intended compositions are deliberately non-unitary:
60	
61	- Get one paper: plan and acquire, or import a configured local file, then stop at `acquisition.json`.
62	- Prepare one source: acquire/import if needed, select artifact or DOI metadata, then materialize the same
63	  `article.json` contract.
64	- Refresh a catalog: rebuild `inventory.jsonl` across any pre-existing direct-child articles, without
65	  reacquiring or unpacking them.
66	
67	The first source-ready publication freezes both metadata mode and PDF inclusion. Later acquisition can add
68	staged forms, but materialization refuses any form-set change that would mutate the immutable article.
69	Interrupted pre-sentinel components are validated and adopted on retry; private tree scratch is swept under
70	the source lease. Inventory replacement is generation-pinned and requires an explicit `force` request.
71	
72	A PDF-only acquisition can be receipted and can motivate an explicit DOI lookup, but the current
73	materializer does not mint `article.json` from a lone PDF. That route requires a separately versioned PDF
74	validation profile rather than an exception to the LaTeX source-ready contract.
75
```

1	# Python project scaffolding for codex-scientiae.
2	#
3	# This repository is mixed-language; Python is one lane. This file exists to make that lane's
4	# package importable under ONE name, not to publish a distribution.
5	#
6	# The problem it solves: jsonl_engine must be reachable under one import path -- `jsonl_engine` for
7	# both tests and `python -m`. Two names for one package means two module objects if both load, which
8	# duplicates module-level state such as the global SchemaRegistry.
9	#
10	#     .venv/Scripts/python.exe -m pip install -r requirements.txt
11	#     .venv/Scripts/python.exe -m pip install -e .
12	#
13	# Dependencies are not declared here. requirements.txt is this repository's environment manifest and
14	# stays the single source; duplicating the list would recreate the same fork one layer up.
15	#
16	# Hatchling rather than setuptools: setuptools writes a .egg-info directory into the source tree,
17	# and redirecting it with egg_base only moves the problem, since the target must be tracked for a
18	# fresh clone to install. Hatchling emits none -- an editable install is a .pth file and nothing
19	# else. It also ships non-Python files inside the package by default, so schemas/*.schema.json
20	# travel with the engine without a package-data declaration.
21	
22	[build-system]
23	requires = ["hatchling"]
24	build-backend = "hatchling.build"
25	
26	[project]
27	name = "codex-scientiae"
28	version = "0.1.0"
29	description = "Codex Scientiae — Python lane for artifact registries and scholarly procurement"
30	requires-python = ">=3.12"
31	
32	[project.scripts]
33	scientiae-procurement = "procurement_mcp.server:main"
34	
35	[tool.hatch.build.targets.wheel]
36	only-include = [
37	    "src/jsonl_engine",
38	    "src/procurement",
39	    "src/mcp-servers/procurement_mcp",
40	]
41	
42	[tool.hatch.build.targets.wheel.sources]
43	"src/jsonl_engine" = "jsonl_engine"
44	"src/procurement" = "procurement"
45	"src/mcp-servers/procurement_mcp" = "procurement_mcp"
46	
47	[tool.pytest.ini_options]
48	# Anchors rootdir at the repository root so `pytest` and `python -m pytest` agree. Import resolution
49	# does not depend on the working directory -- that comes from the editable install, not from here --
50	# so the suite passes from any directory, including outside the repository.
51	#
52	# testpaths is honoured only when pytest is invoked FROM the rootdir; from a subdirectory pytest
53	# collects that subtree instead, which is its intended behaviour.
54	testpaths = ["tests"]
55	python_files = ["test_*.py"]

153	            catalogs=catalog_service,
154	            materialization=materialization_service,
155	        )
156	    except BaseException:
157	        roots.close()
158	        raise

160	
161	def _validate_composition_settings(
162	    settings: DiscoverySettings,
163	    factories: ProviderFactoryCatalog,
164	) -> None:
165	    """Fail before runtime allocation when composition data cannot form the application."""
166	
167	    configured = set(settings.providers)
168	    noncanonical = sorted(
169	        name for name in configured if not name or name != name.casefold()
170	    )
171	    if noncanonical:
172	        raise ConfigurationError(
173	            "provider configuration keys must be canonical lowercase names: "
174	            + ", ".join(noncanonical)
175	        )
176	    try:
177	        factories.validate_settings(settings.providers)
178	    except ValueError as exc:
179	        raise ConfigurationError(str(exc)) from exc
180	
181	    declared = {definition.name: definition for definition in factories.definitions()}
182	
183	    defaults = _canonical_provider_sequence(settings.default_sources, label="default_sources")
184	    if not defaults:
185	        raise ConfigurationError("default_sources must not be empty")
186	    if len(defaults) != len(set(defaults)):
187	        raise ConfigurationError("default_sources must not contain duplicates")
188	    unknown_defaults = set(defaults).difference(declared)
189	    if unknown_defaults:
190	        raise ConfigurationError(
191	            "default_sources contain unknown providers: "
192	            + ", ".join(sorted(unknown_defaults))
193	        )
194	    invalid_defaults = {
195	        name
196	        for name in defaults
197	        if Capability.SEARCH not in declared[name].capabilities
198	    }
199	    if invalid_defaults:
200	        raise ConfigurationError(
201	            "default_sources contain non-search providers: "
202	            + ", ".join(sorted(invalid_defaults))
203	        )
204	
205	    fallbacks = _canonical_provider_sequence(
206	        settings.metadata_fallback_sources,
207	        label="metadata_fallback_sources",
208	    )
209	    if not fallbacks:
210	        raise ConfigurationError("metadata_fallback_sources must not be empty")
211	    if len(fallbacks) != len(set(fallbacks)):
212	        raise ConfigurationError("metadata_fallback_sources must not contain duplicates")
213	    unknown_fallbacks = set(fallbacks).difference(declared)
214	    if unknown_fallbacks:
215	        raise ConfigurationError(
216	            "metadata_fallback_sources contain unknown providers: "
217	            + ", ".join(sorted(unknown_fallbacks))
218	        )
219	    invalid_fallbacks = {
220	        name
221	        for name in fallbacks
222	        if ProviderRole.METADATA_AGGREGATOR not in declared[name].roles
223	    }
224	    if invalid_fallbacks:
225	        raise ConfigurationError(
226	            "metadata fallbacks must be metadata aggregators: "
227	            + ", ".join(sorted(invalid_fallbacks))
228	        )
229	
230	    catalogs = [catalog.name.casefold() for catalog in settings.acquisition.catalogs]
231	    if not catalogs:
232	        raise ConfigurationError("acquisition settings require at least one catalog")
233	    if len(catalogs) != len(set(catalogs)):
234	        raise ConfigurationError("acquisition catalog names must be unique")
235	
236	    inboxes = [inbox.name.casefold() for inbox in settings.acquisition.local_inboxes]
237	    if not inboxes:
238	        raise ConfigurationError("acquisition settings require at least one local inbox")
239	    if len(inboxes) != len(set(inboxes)):
240	        raise ConfigurationError("acquisition local inbox names must be unique")
241	
242	
243	def _canonical_provider_sequence(values: tuple[str, ...], *, label: str) -> tuple[str, ...]:
244	    """Validate ordered provider names without silently normalizing configuration."""
245	
246	    invalid = [name for name in values if not name or name != name.casefold()]
247	    if invalid:
248	        raise ConfigurationError(
249	            f"{label} must contain canonical lowercase provider names: "
250	            + ", ".join(repr(name) for name in invalid)
251	        )
252	    return values
253	
254	
255	def _resolve_workspace_root(value: str | Path | None) -> Path:
256	    """Resolve one physical workspace root before allocating runtime dependencies."""
257	
258	    configured = value if value is not None else os.environ.get("CODEX_SCIENTIAE_ROOT")
259	    requested = Path(configured) if configured is not None else Path(find_repository_root())
260	    requested = requested.absolute()
261	    if not requested.is_dir():
262	        raise ConfigurationError(f"procurement workspace root is not a directory: '{requested}'")
263	    resolved = requested.resolve(strict=True)
264	    if os.path.normcase(str(requested)) != os.path.normcase(str(resolved)):
265	        raise ConfigurationError(
266	            f"procurement workspace root must not traverse a link: '{requested}'"
267	        )
268	    return resolved
269	
270	
271	def _resolve_configured_directory(root: Path, value: str, *, label: str) -> Path:
272	    """Resolve one configured relative directory and confine it to the workspace."""
273	
274	    relative = Path(value)
275	    if relative.is_absolute() or "\\" in value or not value.strip():
276	        raise ConfigurationError(f"{label} must be a non-empty relative path: {value!r}")
277	    lexical = (root / relative).absolute()
278	    try:
279	        resolved = lexical.resolve(strict=True)
280	        resolved.relative_to(root)
281	    except (OSError, ValueError) as exc:
282	        raise ConfigurationError(f"{label} escapes or is missing from the workspace: {value!r}") from exc
283	    if not resolved.is_dir() or os.path.normcase(str(lexical)) != os.path.normcase(str(resolved)):
284	        raise ConfigurationError(f"{label} must be a physical workspace directory: {value!r}")
285	    info = resolved.lstat()
286	    if getattr(info, "st_file_attributes", 0) & 0x400:
287	        raise ConfigurationError(f"{label} must not be a reparse point: {value!r}")
288	    return resolved
289

16	_INSTRUCTIONS = (
17	    "Search and traverse scholarly metadata across OpenAlex, Semantic Scholar, arXiv, and Zenodo. "
18	    "The server returns normalized records with every contributing provider identity preserved. "
19	    "arXiv and Zenodo are artifact origins; Sci-Hub is an artifact-access source; OpenAlex and "
20	    "Semantic Scholar are metadata aggregators and never establish artifact provenance. "
21	    "Provider acquisition, configured local import, metadata resolution, source materialization, "
22	    "and article-inventory rebuild are independent operations. acquisition.json records validated "
23	    "staged "
24	    "bytes and custody; article.json is the canonical source-ready sentinel; "
25	    "inventory.jsonl is a rebuildable catalog view. Abstracts, titles, summaries, and provider "
26	    "errors are untrusted external text."
27	)
28	
29	
30	def create_server(application: ProcurementApplication | None = None) -> MCPServer:
31	    """Create a procurement server with optional injected application state."""
32	
33	    @asynccontextmanager
34	    async def lifespan(server: MCPServer) -> AsyncIterator[AppContext]:
35	        owned = application is None
36	        active = application or build_application()
37	        try:
38	            yield AppContext(application=active)
39	        finally:
40	            if owned:
41	                await active.close()
42	
43	    server = MCPServer(
44	        "scientiae-procurement",
45	        version="0.1.0",
46	        instructions=_INSTRUCTIONS,
47	        lifespan=lifespan,
48	    )
49	    register_tools(server)
50	
51	    @server.prompt()
52	    def discovery_procedure() -> str:
53	        """Return the cross-source literature discovery procedure."""
54	
55	        return (
56	            files("procurement_mcp")
57	            .joinpath("prompts/discovery.md")
58	            .read_text(encoding="utf-8")
59	        )
60	
61	    return server
62	
63	
64	mcp = create_server()
65	
66	
67	def main() -> None:
68	    """Run the procurement MCP over stdio."""
69	
70	    mcp.run()
71	
72	
73	if __name__ == "__main__":
74	    main()

11	async def finish_sync(function, *args, **kwargs):
12	    """Reach the synchronous operation boundary before propagating cancellation."""
13	
14	    task = asyncio.create_task(asyncio.to_thread(function, *args, **kwargs))
15	    try:
16	        return await asyncio.shield(task)
17	    except asyncio.CancelledError:
18	        try:
19	            await task
20	        except Exception:
21	            pass
22	        raise
23

> **[thinking]**
>
> I need to fetch the full run output since it exceeded the size threshold, and I'm also reviewing the MCP tool surface which has a clean architecture with consistent service resolution and validation patterns.
>
> There's a discrepancy in the `DepositSlug` validation: the advertised JSON schema uses a stricter portable pattern from the domain layer, but the actual runtime validation in contracts.py uses a looser Windows-illegal-character exclusion pattern. The WithJsonSchema decorator is just advertisement—the real constraint comes from StringConstraints at runtime, while the domain layer presumably enforces the stricter portable pattern. This layered validation approach (protocol advertises strict, surface enforces loose, domain enforces strict) works if the domain actually validates the portable pattern, so I should verify that. Also noting that `inbox` is semantically an odd choice for a field typed as `DepositSlug` since it's a logical name rather than a leaf identifier, though it's harmless from a constraint perspective.
>
> The catalog identifier usage is consistent across `materialize_source_deposit` and the tools in catalogs.py—both use `NonEmptyIdentifier`. The `rebuild_article_inventory` flow calls `service.resolve(catalog)` twice (once after rebuild, once for the canonical name via descriptor), which is fine since the result already carries the catalog directory. Discovery tools don't need None-guards because discovery and metadata are required fields in ProcurementApplication, maintaining coherence with the dataclass definition where providers, discovery, metadata, http, and roots are mandatory while acquisition, local_import, catalogs, and materialization are optional.
>
> The `finish_sync` helper is used for inspection and metadata operations like `get_acquisition_receipt`, `prepare_article_metadata_by_doi`, `inspect_article_catalog`, and `rebuild_article_inventory`, but not for the async service methods like `acquire_artifact`, `materialize_source_deposit`, or `import_local_artifact`—those handle threading internally via `asyncio.to_thread`.
>
> The key concern is cancellation safety: `finish_sync` shields the sync boundary from cancellation, but if the async service methods don't shield their internal `to_thread` calls, a client cancellation could propagate while the background thread continues running detached, leaving a mid-transaction write orphaned. I need to verify how the async service methods handle cancellation in their implementations.
>
> Looking at the server setup, `mcp = create_server()` at module scope just instantiates the MCPServer and registers tools—it's lightweight and fine for module import. The actual application building happens in the lifespan context. The module-global `mcp` probably exists for tooling like `mcp dev`. On the import side, I'm checking whether the SDK uses `MCPServer` or the older `FastMCP` class name.…

> **[thinking]**
>
> I've reviewed the MCP surface and found it clean overall, but identified two verification targets: DepositSlug's JSON schema advertises a stricter pattern than its runtime validation, and three artifact services may lack proper cancellation shielding. Now I'll fetch the full diagnostics journal and check the provider-architecture layer.

90	        object.__setattr__(self, "name", name)

95	        except (TypeError, ValueError) as exc:

97	        constraints = frozenset(self.search_constraints)

50	        roles: frozenset[ProviderRole] = frozenset(),
51	    ) -> None:
52	        if descriptor is None:
53	            declaration = getattr(provider, "descriptor", None)
54	            if not isinstance(declaration, ProviderDefinition):
55	                raise ValueError("a provider requires an immutable descriptor declaration")
56	        elif isinstance(descriptor, ProviderDefinition):

59	            declaration = descriptor

62	            normalized_roles = frozenset(ProviderRole(role) for role in roles)

67	                roles=normalized_roles,

69	            )

92	        if constraints != self.descriptor.search_constraints:

95	            )

118	class ProviderCatalog:

120	
121	    def __init__(self, bindings: tuple[ProviderBinding, ...] | list[ProviderBinding] = ()) -> None:
122	        self._bindings: dict[str, ProviderBinding] = {}
123	        for binding in bindings:
124	            self.register(binding)
125	
126	    def register(self, binding: ProviderBinding) -> None:
127	        key = binding.name.casefold()
128	        if key in self._bindings:
129	            raise ValueError(f"provider already registered: {binding.name}")
130	        self._bindings[key] = binding
131	
132	    def get(self, name: str, capability: Capability) -> Any:
133	        binding = self.binding(name)
134	        if capability not in binding.capabilities:
135	            raise UnsupportedCapabilityError(
136	                f"provider {binding.name!r} does not support {capability.value!r}"
137	            )
138	        return binding.provider
139	
140	    def bindings(
141	        self,
142	        *,
143	        capability: Capability | None = None,
144	        role: ProviderRole | None = None,
145	        category: ProviderCategory | None = None,
146	    ) -> tuple[ProviderBinding, ...]:
147	        """Return declarations matching every supplied selector."""
148	
149	        return tuple(
150	            binding
151	            for binding in self._bindings.values()
152	            if (capability is None or capability in binding.capabilities)
153	            and (role is None or role in binding.roles)
154	            and (category is None or category is binding.category)
155	        )
156

158	        self,
159	        capability: Capability | None = None,
160	        *,
161	        role: ProviderRole | None = None,
162	        category: ProviderCategory | None = None,
163	    ) -> tuple[str, ...]:
164	        return tuple(
165	            binding.name
166	            for binding in self.bindings(
167	                capability=capability,
168	                role=role,
169	                category=category,
170	            )
171	        )
172	
173	    def binding(self, name: str) -> ProviderBinding:
174	        """Return one complete declaration by case-insensitive name."""
175	
176	        binding = self._bindings.get(name.casefold())
177	        if binding is None:
178	            known = ", ".join(self.names()) or "(none)"
179	            raise ProviderNotFoundError(f"unknown provider {name!r}; registered providers: {known}")
180	        return binding
181	
182	    def describe(self) -> tuple[ProviderDescriptor, ...]:
183	        """Return stable declarations for every provider."""
184	
185	        return tuple(
186	            ProviderDescriptor(

188	                category=binding.category.value,
189	                roles=tuple(sorted(role.value for role in binding.roles)),
190	                capabilities=tuple(sorted(capability.value for capability in binding.capabilities)),
191	                search_constraints=tuple(sorted(binding.search_constraints)),
192	            )
193	            for binding in self._bindings.values()

1	"""Shared immutable value-model primitives."""
2	
3	from __future__ import annotations
4	
5	from collections.abc import Mapping
6	from typing import Annotated, Any
7	
8	from pydantic import AfterValidator, BaseModel, ConfigDict, PlainSerializer
9	
10	
11	def _unique_strings(values: Any) -> tuple[str, ...]:
12	    if values is None:
13	        return ()
14	    if isinstance(values, str):
15	        values = (values,)
16	    seen: set[str] = set()
17	    result: list[str] = []
18	    for value in values:
19	        if value is None:
20	            continue
21	        text = str(value).strip()
22	        key = text.casefold()
23	        if text and key not in seen:
24	            seen.add(key)
25	            result.append(text)
26	    return tuple(result)
27	
28	
29	class FrozenStringMapping(Mapping[str, str]):
30	    """Immutable, deepcopy-stable string mapping."""
31	
32	    __slots__ = ("_data",)
33	
34	    def __init__(self, value: Mapping[str, str]) -> None:
35	        self._data = dict(value)
36	
37	    def __getitem__(self, key: str) -> str:
38	        return self._data[key]
39	
40	    def __iter__(self):
41	        return iter(self._data)
42	
43	    def __len__(self) -> int:
44	        return len(self._data)
45	
46	    def __repr__(self) -> str:
47	        return f"FrozenStringMapping({self._data!r})"
48	
49	    def __eq__(self, other: object) -> bool:
50	        return isinstance(other, Mapping) and dict(self.items()) == dict(other.items())
51	
52	    def __copy__(self) -> "FrozenStringMapping":
53	        return self
54	
55	    def __deepcopy__(self, memo: dict[int, object]) -> "FrozenStringMapping":
56	        return self
57	
58	
59	def _freeze_string_map(value: Mapping[str, str]) -> Mapping[str, str]:
60	    if isinstance(value, FrozenStringMapping):
61	        return value
62	    return FrozenStringMapping(value)
63	
64	
65	FrozenStringMap = Annotated[
66	    Mapping[str, str],
67	    AfterValidator(_freeze_string_map),
68	    PlainSerializer(lambda value: dict(value), return_type=dict[str, str]),
69	]
70	
71	
72	def _require_serialized_properties(schema: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
73	    """Mark every default-serialized object property as required."""
74	
75	    properties = schema.get("properties")
76	    if isinstance(properties, dict):
77	        schema["required"] = list(properties)
78	
79

81	    """Immutable, extra-forbidding base for public procurement values."""

86	        populate_by_name=True,
87	        serialize_by_alias=True,
88	        validate_default=True,
89	        json_schema_serialization_defaults_required=True,
90	        json_schema_extra=_require_serialized_properties,
91	    )

41	class ProviderFactory:

43	
44	    adapter_type: type[Any]
45	    constructor: ProviderConstructor
46	    requires_settings: bool = True

50	    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
51	        descriptor = getattr(self.adapter_type, "descriptor", None)
52	        if not isinstance(descriptor, ProviderDefinition):
53	            raise ValueError("provider adapter types require an immutable descriptor")
54	        object.__setattr__(self, "descriptor", descriptor)
55	        if not callable(self.constructor):

57	        if not self.requires_settings and self.settings_validator is not None:

89	    def configured(
90	        cls,
91	        adapter_type: type[Any],
92	        *,
93	        artifact_limits: bool = False,
94	        settings_validator: ProviderSettingsValidator | None = None,
95	    ) -> "ProviderFactory":
96	        """Declare an adapter with the standard configured constructor shape."""
97	
98	        descriptor = getattr(adapter_type, "descriptor", None)
99	        name = descriptor.name if isinstance(descriptor, ProviderDefinition) else "provider"
100	
101	        if artifact_limits:
102	
103	            def construct(context: ProviderBuildContext) -> Any:
104	                return adapter_type(
105	                    context.http,
106	                    context.require_settings(name),
107	                    context.secrets,
108	                    context.artifact_limits,
109	                )
110	
111	        else:
112	
113	            def construct(context: ProviderBuildContext) -> Any:
114	                return adapter_type(
115	                    context.http,
116	                    context.require_settings(name),
117	                    context.secrets,
118	                )
119	
120	        return cls(
121	            adapter_type=adapter_type,
122	            constructor=construct,
123	            requires_settings=True,
124	            settings_validator=settings_validator,
125	        )
126	
127	    @classmethod
128	    def declaration_only(cls, adapter_type: type[Any]) -> "ProviderFactory":
129	        """Declare an adapter that has no configured runtime dependencies."""
130	
131	        def construct(_context: ProviderBuildContext) -> Any:
132	            return adapter_type()
133	
134	        return cls(
135	            adapter_type=adapter_type,
136	            constructor=construct,
137	            requires_settings=False,
138	        )
139	
140	
141	@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True, init=False)
142	class ProviderFactoryCatalog:
143	    """Immutable ordered catalog of provider construction declarations."""
144	
145	    _factories: tuple[ProviderFactory, ...]
146	    _by_name: Mapping[str, ProviderFactory]
147	
148	    def __init__(self, factories: Iterable[ProviderFactory] = ()) -> None:
149	        ordered = tuple(factories)
150	        by_name: dict[str, ProviderFactory] = {}
151	        for factory in ordered:
152	            if not isinstance(factory, ProviderFactory):
153	                raise TypeError("provider factory catalogs contain ProviderFactory values")
154	            if factory.name in by_name:
155	                raise ValueError(f"provider factory already declared: {factory.name}")
156	            by_name[factory.name] = factory
157	        object.__setattr__(self, "_factories", ordered)
158	        object.__setattr__(self, "_by_name", MappingProxyType(by_name))
159	
160	    def factories(self) -> tuple[ProviderFactory, ...]:
161	        return self._factories
162	
163	    def names(self, *, requires_settings: bool | None = None) -> tuple[str, ...]:
164	        return tuple(
165	            factory.name
166	            for factory in self._factories
167	            if requires_settings is None or factory.requires_settings is requires_settings
168	        )
169	
170	    def factory(self, name: str) -> ProviderFactory:
171	        factory = self._by_name.get(name.casefold())
172	        if factory is None:
173	            known = ", ".join(self.names()) or "(none)"
174	            raise ValueError(f"unknown provider factory {name!r}; declared providers: {known}")
175	        return factory
176	
177	    def definition(self, name: str) -> ProviderDefinition:
178	        return self.factory(name).descriptor
179	
180	    def definitions(self) -> tuple[ProviderDefinition, ...]:
181	        return tuple(factory.descriptor for factory in self._factories)
182	
183	    def extended(self, *factories: ProviderFactory) -> "ProviderFactoryCatalog":
184	        """Return a new catalog with additional declarations appended."""
185	
186	        return ProviderFactoryCatalog((*self._factories, *factories))
187	
188	    def validate_settings(self, settings: Mapping[str, ProviderHttpSettings]) -> None:
189	        """Validate exact configured-provider coverage and adapter-specific settings."""
190	
191	        configured = set(settings)
192	        expected = set(self.names(requires_settings=True))
193	        unsupported = configured.difference(expected)
194	        if unsupported:
195	            raise ValueError(
196	                "provider settings name adapters without implementations: "
197	                + ", ".join(sorted(unsupported))
198	            )
199	        missing = expected.difference(configured)
200	        if missing:
201	            raise ValueError(
202	                f"discovery settings omit providers: {', '.join(sorted(missing))}"
203	            )
204	        for factory in self._factories:
205	            factory.validate_settings(settings.get(factory.name))
206	
207	    def build(
208	        self,
209	        settings: Mapping[str, ProviderHttpSettings],
210	        *,
211	        http: HttpClient,
212	        secrets: RuntimeSecrets,
213	        artifact_limits: ArtifactLimitSettings,
214	    ) -> ProviderCatalog:
215	        """Construct the runtime catalog from validated settings and dependencies."""
216	
217	        self.validate_settings(settings)
218	        bindings = []
219	        for factory in self._factories:
220	            context = ProviderBuildContext(
221	                http=http,
222	                settings=settings.get(factory.name),
223	                secrets=secrets,
224	                artifact_limits=artifact_limits,
225	            )
226	            bindings.append(factory.bind(context))
227	        return ProviderCatalog(bindings)
228

9	from procurement.errors import IdentifierError

11	_DOI_PREFIX = re.compile(r"^(?:(?:https?://)?(?:dx\.)?doi\.org/|doi:)\s*", re.IGNORECASE)
12	_DOI_IN_TEXT = re.compile(r"10\.\d{4,9}/[^\s<>\"']+", re.IGNORECASE)
13	_DOI_EXACT = re.compile(r"^10\.\d{4,9}/[^\s<>\"']+$", re.IGNORECASE)
14	_ARXIV_NEW = re.compile(r"^\d{2}(?:0[1-9]|1[0-2])\.\d{4,5}(?:v[1-9]\d*)?$", re.IGNORECASE)
15	_ARXIV_OLD = re.compile(
16	    r"^[a-z][a-z-]*(?:\.[a-z]{2})?/\d{2}(?:0[1-9]|1[0-2])\d{3}(?:v[1-9]\d*)?$",
17	    re.IGNORECASE,
18	)
19	_ARXIV_VERSION = re.compile(r"v([1-9]\d*)$", re.IGNORECASE)
20	_ZENODO = re.compile(r"^(?:(?:10\.5281/)?zenodo\.)?(\d+)$", re.IGNORECASE)
21	
22	
23	def normalize_doi(value: object | None) -> str | None:
24	    """Return the lowercase DOI payload without a resolver prefix."""
25	
26	    if value is None:
27	        return None
28	    text = unquote(str(value)).strip()
29	    if not text:
30	        return None
31	    text = _DOI_PREFIX.sub("", text).strip()
32	    return text.casefold() or None
33	
34	
35	def extract_doi(value: object | None) -> str | None:
36	    """Extract and normalize the first DOI-shaped token in text."""
37	
38	    if value is None:
39	        return None
40	    match = _DOI_IN_TEXT.search(str(value))
41	    if not match:
42	        return None
43	    candidate = match.group(0).rstrip(".,;:)]}")
44	    return normalize_doi(candidate)
45	
46	
47	def is_doi(value: object | None) -> bool:
48	    """Return whether a value is a complete DOI in a supported resolver form."""
49	
50	    normalized = normalize_doi(value)
51	    return bool(normalized and _DOI_EXACT.fullmatch(normalized))
52	
53	
54	def normalize_arxiv_id(value: object | None) -> str | None:
55	    """Return a bare arXiv identifier, preserving a supplied version."""
56	
57	    if value is None:
58	        return None
59	    text = unquote(str(value)).strip()
60	    if not text:
61	        return None
62	    text = re.sub(r"^arxiv:\s*", "", text, flags=re.IGNORECASE)
63	    if text.lower().startswith(("http://", "https://")):
64	        path = urlparse(text).path
65	        match = re.search(r"/(?:abs|pdf|html|src)/(.*)$", path, flags=re.IGNORECASE)
66	        if match:
67	            text = match.group(1)
68	    text = re.sub(r"\.pdf$", "", text, flags=re.IGNORECASE).strip("/")
69	    return text if is_arxiv_id(text) else None
70	
71	
72	def is_arxiv_id(value: object | None) -> bool:
73	    """Return whether a value is a modern or legacy arXiv identifier."""
74	
75	    if value is None:
76	        return False
77	    text = str(value).strip()
78	    return bool(_ARXIV_NEW.fullmatch(text) or _ARXIV_OLD.fullmatch(text))

80	
81	@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
82	class ArxivIdentifier:
83	    """Versioned and versionless forms of an arXiv identifier."""
84	
85	    versionless: str
86	    versioned: str
87	    version: int | None
88	
89	
90	def split_arxiv_id(value: object) -> ArxivIdentifier:
91	    """Split an arXiv identifier into stable and versioned forms."""
92	
93	    normalized = normalize_arxiv_id(value)
94	    if normalized is None:
95	        raise IdentifierError(f"invalid arXiv identifier: {value!r}")
96	    match = _ARXIV_VERSION.search(normalized)
97	    if not match:
98	        return ArxivIdentifier(normalized, normalized, None)
99	    return ArxivIdentifier(normalized[: match.start()], normalized, int(match.group(1)))
100	
101	
102	def arxiv_identity(value: object) -> str:
103	    """Return the case-insensitive versionless arXiv identity key."""
104	
105	    return split_arxiv_id(value).versionless.casefold()
106	
107	
108	def arxiv_deposit_slug(value: object) -> str:
109	    """Return the portable deposit leaf for one arXiv identifier."""
110	
111	    return split_arxiv_id(value).versioned.replace("/", "_")
112	
113	
114	@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
115	class ZenodoIdentifier:
116	    """Canonical forms of a Zenodo record identifier."""
117	
118	    record_id: str
119	    doi: str
120	    slug: str
121	
122	
123	def split_zenodo_id(value: object) -> ZenodoIdentifier:
124	    """Parse a Zenodo record number, shorthand, or DOI."""
125	
126	    text = unquote(str(value)).strip()
127	    parsed = urlparse(text)
128	    if parsed.hostname and parsed.hostname.casefold() in {"zenodo.org", "www.zenodo.org"}:
129	        match = re.fullmatch(r"/(?:record|records)/(\d+)/?", parsed.path, re.IGNORECASE)
130	        if match:
131	            text = match.group(1)
132	    else:
133	        text = re.sub(r"^doi:\s*", "", text, flags=re.IGNORECASE)
134	        text = re.sub(
135	            r"^https?://(?:dx\.)?doi\.org/",
136	            "",
137	            text,
138	            flags=re.IGNORECASE,
139	        )
140	    match = _ZENODO.fullmatch(text)
141	    if not match:
142	        raise IdentifierError(f"invalid Zenodo identifier: {value!r}")
143	    record_number = int(match.group(1))
144	    if record_number < 1:
145	        raise IdentifierError(f"invalid Zenodo identifier: {value!r}")
146	    record_id = str(record_number)
147	    return ZenodoIdentifier(
148	        record_id=record_id,
149	        doi=f"10.5281/zenodo.{record_id}",
150	        slug=f"zenodo_{record_id}",
151	    )
152	
153	
154	def artifact_slug(provider: str, identifier: object) -> str:
155	    """Return the canonical portable leaf for an artifact-provider identity."""
156	
157	    key = provider.casefold()
158	    if key == "arxiv":
159	        return arxiv_deposit_slug(identifier)
160	    if key == "zenodo":
161	        return split_zenodo_id(identifier).slug
162	    if key == "scihub":
163	        doi = normalize_doi(identifier)
164	        if not is_doi(doi):
165	            raise IdentifierError(f"invalid DOI artifact identifier: {identifier!r}")
166	        slug = re.sub(r"[^A-Za-z0-9._-]", "_", doi or "").strip("_")
167	        if not slug:
168	            raise IdentifierError(f"invalid DOI artifact identifier: {identifier!r}")
169	        return slug
170	    raise IdentifierError(f"unknown artifact provider: {provider!r}")

> **[thinking]**
>
> The test suite ran cleanly with 429 passed tests, 10 skipped, and 140 subtests passing—the earlier failures were entirely due to a corrupted temporary directory that was poisoning subsequent runs. The skips are legitimate platform-specific cases, mostly Windows symlink privilege issues and a couple of intentional Windows-specific alternates. That poisoned temp directory will continue breaking future pytest runs across the system unless cleaned up.
>
> Looking at the codebase, factory.py enforces strict validation—configured settings must exactly match what each factory declares it requires, so any mismatch (missing or extra settings) throws an error. This means configuration and code have to stay in sync. Sci-Hub is deliberately configured to take no settings at all, which means you can't configure its HTTP behavior without modifying the builtin code directly. Checking scihub.py confirms it's purely a declaration with no actual acquisition capabilities—the Python implementation only handles identity and slug support through the identifiers module.
>
> The identifiers module handles normalization cleanly: DOI casefold, arXiv old/new formats, zenodo forms. Zenodo's a bit loose—it accepts bare digit strings as IDs via split_zenodo_id, but that's only used where zenodo context is already explicit, so it's safe. In configuration/models.py, I'm noticing some asymmetries: inbox names get uniqueness validation in both the model validator and composition layer, while catalog names only get checked in composition. Similarly, inbox names validate against deposit slug rules but catalog names don't have portable-name validation, even though catalogs appear in file paths. The dependencies confirm httpx, pydantic v2.13+, and defusedxml for secure XML parsing, with mcp pinned to version 2. The critical files I need to examine are transport/http.py, storage/roots.py, and the acquisition/planning logic — those are where the real complexity lives. I'm thinking about whether to have agy do a full deep dive or combine the analysis into one turn to manage costs.
>

This way agy can resume cleanly from where the first turn ends.
>
> For Turn A, I'm setting up as an adjutant reviewer working through evidence lanes while the primary agent handles architecture. The test suite is solid—429 passed with 10 skipped (Windows symlink issues) and zero failures after fixing the temp directory issue. I have the full test census and collection data ready. My first job is mapping every module in the procurement package and MCP server to which tests exercise it, then flagging anything with missing or thin coverage, especially the transport layer's HTTP policies around redirect confinement and HTTPS enforcement.
>
> Then I need to audit those 10 skips to see what invariants go unverified on Windows and whether any skip is masking a real platform gap. Finally, I'll spot-check the five most safety-critical test claims to verify they're actually testing what they claim.
>
> The output should be a markdown report with file:line citations, marking each finding as confirmed, inferred, or uncertain, and ending with questions for the primary author. I need to be skeptical about coverage gaps—checking both the test files and the jsonl_engine since materialization delegates publication there. When I'm unsure, I should say so rather than guess.
>
> Now I'm working through the specific claims to verify: article.json published last in the materialization transaction, no-replace atomicity across archive/tree/pdf/inventory, generation pinning ensuring retained descriptors are used for all byte transfer and validation without re-resolution, plans never serialized across MCP with URL-free summaries, non-loopback HTTPS enforcement with no downgrades, bounded downloads with size caps enforced during streaming, SHA-256 local measurement paired with provider-native checks when available, and sync work properly offloaded from the event loop with cancellation safety.

> Looking at duplication across the codebase: validation, hashing, and path-confinement logic appears scattered across storage/acquisitions.py, storage/source_deposits.py, storage/roots.py, and source/_safety.py, and I need to determine if the PowerShell logistics scripts are doing redundant work compared to the procurement materialization and catalog systems.

10	from types import MappingProxyType
11	from typing import Mapping

13	from jsonl_engine.publication import PinnedPublicationRoot
14	
15	
16	class ConfiguredRootError(ValueError):
17	    """Configured root definitions or lifecycle state are invalid."""
18	
19	
20	class ConfiguredRootKind(StrEnum):
21	    """Logical namespaces for procurement-owned filesystem roots."""
22	
23	    STAGING = "staging"
24	    LOCAL_INBOX = "local-inbox"
25	    ARTICLE_CATALOG = "article-catalog"
26	
27	
28	@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
29	class ConfiguredRootDescriptor:
30	    """One logical root bound to an active physical directory generation."""
31	
32	    kind: ConfiguredRootKind
33	    name: str
34	    path: str
35	    identity: tuple[int, ...]
36	    publication_root: PinnedPublicationRoot = field(repr=False, compare=False)
37	
38	    def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, object]:
39	        return {
40	            "kind": self.kind.value,
41	            "name": self.name,
42	            "path": self.path,
43	            "identity": list(self.identity),
44	        }
45	
46	
47	@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
48	class _RootDefinition:
49	    kind: ConfiguredRootKind
50	    name: str
51	    path: str
52	
53	
54	class ProcurementRootCatalog:
55	    """Open and retain every configured procurement root for one application lifetime."""
56	
57	    STAGING_NAME = "default"
58	
59	    def __init__(
60	        self,
61	        staging_root: str | Path,
62	        *,
63	        local_inboxes: Mapping[str, str | Path],
64	        article_catalogs: Mapping[str, str | Path],
65	    ) -> None:
66	        definitions = [
67	            _RootDefinition(
68	                ConfiguredRootKind.STAGING,
69	                self.STAGING_NAME,
70	                self._absolute_path(staging_root, label="staging root"),
71	            )
72	        ]
73	        definitions.extend(
74	            self._mapping_definitions(
75	                ConfiguredRootKind.ARTICLE_CATALOG,
76	                article_catalogs,
77	            )
78	        )
79	        definitions.extend(
80	            self._mapping_definitions(
81	                ConfiguredRootKind.LOCAL_INBOX,
82	                local_inboxes,
83	            )
84	        )
85	
86	        path_keys: dict[str, _RootDefinition] = {}
87	        for definition in definitions:
88	            key = os.path.normcase(definition.path)
89	            prior = path_keys.get(key)
90	            if prior is not None:
91	                raise ConfiguredRootError(
92	                    "configured roots must not alias one lexical directory: "
93	                    f"{prior.kind.value}/{prior.name} and "
94	                    f"{definition.kind.value}/{definition.name} both name "
95	                    f"'{definition.path}'"
96	                )
97	            path_keys[key] = definition
98	
99	        self._definitions = tuple(definitions)
100	        self._stack: ExitStack | None = None
101	        self._descriptors: Mapping[
102	            tuple[ConfiguredRootKind, str], ConfiguredRootDescriptor
103	        ] = MappingProxyType({})
104	
105	    @staticmethod
106	    def _absolute_path(value: str | Path, *, label: str) -> str:
107	        if not isinstance(value, (str, os.PathLike)):
108	            raise ConfiguredRootError(f"{label} must be a non-empty filesystem path")
109	        raw = os.fspath(value)
110	        if not isinstance(raw, str) or not raw.strip():
111	            raise ConfiguredRootError(f"{label} must be a non-empty text filesystem path")
112	        return os.path.abspath(raw)
113	
114	    @classmethod
115	    def _mapping_definitions(
116	        cls,
117	        kind: ConfiguredRootKind,
118	        values: Mapping[str, str | Path],
119	    ) -> list[_RootDefinition]:
120	        if not values:
121	            raise ConfiguredRootError(f"at least one {kind.value} root is required")
122	        configured: dict[str, _RootDefinition] = {}
123	        for name, path in values.items():
124	            if not isinstance(name, str) or not name or name != name.strip():
125	                raise ConfiguredRootError(
126	                    f"{kind.value} names must be non-empty strings without surrounding whitespace"
127	                )
128	            key = name.casefold()
129	            if key in configured:
130	                raise ConfiguredRootError(
131	                    f"{kind.value} names contain a case collision at {name!r}"
132	                )
133	            configured[key] = _RootDefinition(
134	                kind,
135	                name,
136	                cls._absolute_path(path, label=f"{kind.value} {name!r}"),
137	            )
138	        return sorted(configured.values(), key=lambda item: item.name.casefold())
139	
140	    @property
141	    def is_open(self) -> bool:
142	        """Return whether every configured root is actively retained."""
143	
144	        return self._stack is not None
145	
146	    def open(self) -> "ProcurementRootCatalog":
147	        """Pin every configured directory and capture its physical identity."""
148	
149	        if self._stack is not None:
150	            raise ConfiguredRootError("configured root catalog is already open")
151	        stack = ExitStack()
152	        descriptors: dict[tuple[ConfiguredRootKind, str], ConfiguredRootDescriptor] = {}
153	        physical: dict[tuple[int, ...], _RootDefinition] = {}
154	        try:
155	            for definition in self._definitions:
156	                root = stack.enter_context(PinnedPublicationRoot(definition.path))
157	                identity = root.identity
158	                prior = physical.get(identity)
159	                if prior is not None:
160	                    raise ConfiguredRootError(
161	                        "configured roots must not alias one physical directory generation: "
162	                        f"{prior.kind.value}/{prior.name} and "
163	                        f"{definition.kind.value}/{definition.name}"
164	                    )
165	                physical[identity] = definition
166	                descriptors[(definition.kind, definition.name.casefold())] = (
167	                    ConfiguredRootDescriptor(
168	                        kind=definition.kind,
169	                        name=definition.name,
170	                        path=definition.path,
171	                        identity=identity,
172	                        publication_root=root,
173	                    )
174	                )
175	        except BaseException:
176	            stack.close()
177	            raise
178	        self._descriptors = MappingProxyType(descriptors)
179	        self._stack = stack
180	        return self
181	
182	    def close(self) -> None:
183	        """Release every configured root handle or descriptor."""
184	
185	        stack = self._stack
186	        self._stack = None
187	        if stack is not None:
188	            stack.close()
189	
190	    def __enter__(self) -> "ProcurementRootCatalog":
191	        return self.open()
192	
193	    def __exit__(self, exc_type: object, exc: object, traceback: object) -> None:
194	        self.close()
195	
196	    def _require_open(self) -> None:
197	        if self._stack is None:
198	            raise ConfiguredRootError("configured root catalog is not open")
199	
200	    def resolve(
201	        self,
202	        kind: ConfiguredRootKind | str,
203	        name: str,
204	    ) -> ConfiguredRootDescriptor:
205	        """Return one active descriptor by namespace and case-insensitive logical name."""
206	
207	        self._require_open()
208	        try:
209	            resolved_kind = ConfiguredRootKind(kind)
210	        except ValueError as exc:
211	            raise ConfiguredRootError(f"unknown configured root kind: {kind!r}") from exc
212	        if not isinstance(name, str) or not name.strip():
213	            raise ConfiguredRootError("configured root name must not be blank")
214	        descriptor = self._descriptors.get((resolved_kind, name.strip().casefold()))
215	        if descriptor is None:
216	            available = [
217	                item.name for item in self.descriptors(resolved_kind)
218	            ]
219	            raise ConfiguredRootError(
220	                f"unknown {resolved_kind.value} root {name!r}; available: {available}"
221	            )
222	        return descriptor
223	
224	    @property
225	    def staging(self) -> ConfiguredRootDescriptor:
226	        """Return the active acquisition staging root."""
227	
228	        return self.resolve(ConfiguredRootKind.STAGING, self.STAGING_NAME)
229	
230	    def descriptors(
231	        self,
232	        kind: ConfiguredRootKind | str | None = None,
233	    ) -> tuple[ConfiguredRootDescriptor, ...]:
234	        """Return active descriptors in namespace and logical-name order."""
235	
236	        self._require_open()
237	        selected = tuple(self._descriptors.values())
238	        if kind is not None:
239	            try:
240	                resolved_kind = ConfiguredRootKind(kind)
241	            except ValueError as exc:
242	                raise ConfiguredRootError(f"unknown configured root kind: {kind!r}") from exc
243	            selected = tuple(item for item in selected if item.kind is resolved_kind)
244	        return tuple(sorted(selected, key=lambda item: (item.kind.value, item.name.casefold())))
245	
246	    def assert_current(self) -> None:
247	        """Require every configured path to name its retained physical generation."""
248	
249	        self._require_open()
250	        for descriptor in self.descriptors():
251	            descriptor.publication_root.assert_current()
252	
253	
254	__all__ = [
255	    "ConfiguredRootDescriptor",
256	    "ConfiguredRootError",
257	    "ConfiguredRootKind",
258	    "ProcurementRootCatalog",
259	]

1	"""Portable source-deposit identity validation."""
2	
3	from __future__ import annotations
4	
5	from procurement.identifiers import (
6	    artifact_slug,
7	    is_doi,
8	    normalize_doi,
9	    split_arxiv_id,
10	    split_zenodo_id,
11	)
12	
13	_INVALID_PORTABLE_LEAF = frozenset('<>:"/\\|?*')
14	_WINDOWS_RESERVED_LEAVES = frozenset(
15	    ("CON", "PRN", "AUX", "NUL")
16	    + tuple(f"COM{number}" for number in range(1, 10))
17	    + tuple(f"LPT{number}" for number in range(1, 10))
18	)
19	PORTABLE_LEAF_PATTERN = (
20	    r"^(?!(?:[Cc][Oo][Nn]|[Pp][Rr][Nn]|[Aa][Uu][Xx]|[Nn][Uu][Ll]|"
21	    r"[Cc][Oo][Mm][1-9]|[Ll][Pp][Tt][1-9])(?:\.|$))(?!\.{1,2}$)"
22	    r"(?!.*[ .]$)[^<>:\"/\\|?*\u0000-\u001F]+$"
23	)
24	
25	
26	def validate_deposit_slug(value: object) -> str:
27	    """Return one portable deposit-directory leaf."""
28	
29	    if not isinstance(value, str):
30	        raise ValueError("deposit_slug must be a string")
31	    if not value or value in (".", "..") or value[-1] in (" ", "."):
32	        raise ValueError("deposit_slug must be one non-empty portable directory leaf")
33	    if any(ord(char) < 32 or char in _INVALID_PORTABLE_LEAF for char in value):
34	        raise ValueError("deposit_slug must be one non-empty portable directory leaf")
35	    if value.split(".", 1)[0].upper() in _WINDOWS_RESERVED_LEAVES:
36	        raise ValueError("deposit_slug uses a reserved Windows directory name")
37	    return value
38	
39	
40	def validate_artifact_deposit_reference(
41	    provider: str,
42	    deposit_slug: str,
43	    identifier: str,
44	) -> str:
45	    """Validate and canonicalize an artifact identity."""
46	
47	    deposit_slug = validate_deposit_slug(deposit_slug)
48	    key = provider.casefold()
49	    if key == "arxiv":
50	        parsed = split_arxiv_id(identifier)
51	        if parsed.version is None:
52	            raise ValueError("an arXiv source deposit requires a versioned identifier")
53	        expected_slug = artifact_slug("arxiv", parsed.versioned)
54	        if deposit_slug != expected_slug:
55	            raise ValueError(
56	                f"arXiv deposit slug {deposit_slug!r} must match artifact leaf {expected_slug!r}"
57	            )
58	        return parsed.versioned
59	    if key == "zenodo":
60	        parsed = split_zenodo_id(identifier)
61	        if deposit_slug != parsed.slug:
62	            raise ValueError(
63	                f"Zenodo deposit slug {deposit_slug!r} must match artifact {parsed.slug!r}"
64	            )
65	        return parsed.record_id
66	    if key == "scihub":
67	        if not is_doi(identifier):
68	            raise ValueError("Sci-Hub artifact metadata fallback requires a complete DOI")
69	        return normalize_doi(identifier) or ""
70	    if not isinstance(identifier, str) or not identifier.strip():
71	        raise ValueError("artifact identifier must not be empty")
72	    return identifier.strip()
73	
74	
75	__all__ = [
76	    "PORTABLE_LEAF_PATTERN",
77	    "validate_artifact_deposit_reference",
78	    "validate_deposit_slug",
79	]
80

36	def _unique_text(values: object, *, label: str) -> tuple[str, ...]:
37	    if isinstance(values, str):
38	        values = (values,)
39	    if not isinstance(values, (list, tuple, set, frozenset)):
40	        raise ValueError(f"{label} must be a sequence")
41	    result: list[str] = []
42	    seen: set[str] = set()
43	    for value in values:
44	        if not isinstance(value, str) or not value.strip():
45	            raise ValueError(f"{label} entries must be non-empty strings")
46	        text = value.strip()
47	        key = text.casefold()
48	        if key not in seen:
49	            seen.add(key)
50	            result.append(text)
51	    return tuple(result)
52	
53	
54	def _is_loopback_host(value: str) -> bool:
55	    host = value.casefold().rstrip(".")
56	    if host == "localhost":
57	        return True
58	    try:
59	        return ipaddress.ip_address(host).is_loopback
60	    except ValueError:
61	        return False
62

64	def is_safe_artifact_url(value: object) -> bool:
65	    """Return whether an artifact route uses HTTPS or deliberate loopback HTTP."""
66	
67	    if not isinstance(value, str) or not value.strip():
68	        return False
69	    parsed = urlsplit(value.strip())
70	    if not parsed.hostname or parsed.username is not None or parsed.password is not None:
71	        return False
72	    scheme = parsed.scheme.casefold()
73	    return scheme == "https" or (scheme == "http" and _is_loopback_host(parsed.hostname))
74	
75	
76	def _safe_http_url(value: object, *, label: str) -> str:
77	    if not isinstance(value, str) or not value.strip():
78	        raise ValueError(f"{label} must be a non-empty URL")
79	    text = value.strip()
80	    parsed = urlsplit(text)
81	    if not is_safe_artifact_url(text):
82	        raise ValueError(f"{label} must use HTTPS or loopback HTTP without credentials")
83	    if parsed.username is not None or parsed.password is not None:
84	        raise ValueError(f"{label} must not contain URL credentials")
85	    return text

181	class PlannedArtifact(DomainModel):
182	    """Expected bytes and ordered retrieval routes for one logical artifact."""
183	
184	    kind: ArtifactKind
185	    target_leaf: str = Field(
186	        min_length=1,
187	        json_schema_extra={"pattern": PORTABLE_LEAF_PATTERN},

189	    media_type: str = Field(min_length=1)
190	    payload_kind: PayloadKind
191	    minimum_bytes: int = Field(default=1, ge=1)
192	    maximum_bytes: int = Field(ge=1)
193	    expected_bytes: int | None = Field(default=None, ge=1)
194	    checksum: ChecksumExpectation | None = None
195	    candidates: tuple[RetrievalCandidate, ...] = Field(min_length=1)
196	
197	    @field_validator("target_leaf", mode="before")
198	    @classmethod
199	    def _portable_target(cls, value: object) -> str:
200	        return validate_deposit_slug(value)
201	
202	    @model_validator(mode="after")
203	    def _validate_expectation(self) -> Self:
204	        if self.minimum_bytes > self.maximum_bytes:
205	            raise ValueError("minimum_bytes cannot exceed maximum_bytes")
206	        if self.expected_bytes is not None and not (
207	            self.minimum_bytes <= self.expected_bytes <= self.maximum_bytes
208	        ):
209	            raise ValueError("expected_bytes must fall within the payload bounds")
210	        ids = [candidate.candidate_id.casefold() for candidate in self.candidates]
211	        if len(ids) != len(set(ids)):
212	            raise ValueError("retrieval candidate IDs must be unique")
213	        return self

252	    @model_validator(mode="after")
253	    def _validate_plan(self) -> Self:
254	        canonical = validate_artifact_deposit_reference(
255	            self.artifact.provider,
256	            self.deposit_slug,
257	            self.artifact.identifier,
258	        )
259	        if canonical != self.artifact.identifier:
260	            raise ValueError("artifact plan identifier is not canonical")
261	        expected_slug = artifact_slug(self.artifact.provider, self.artifact.identifier)
262	        if self.deposit_slug != expected_slug:
263	            raise ValueError("artifact plan slug does not match its provider identity")
264	        planned = [payload.kind for payload in self.payloads]
265	        missing = [item.kind for item in self.unavailable]
266	        if len(planned) != len(set(planned)) or len(missing) != len(set(missing)):
267	            raise ValueError("planned and unavailable artifact kinds must each be unique")
268	        if set(planned).intersection(missing):
269	            raise ValueError("an artifact kind cannot be both planned and unavailable")
270	        if set(planned).union(missing) != set(self.requested):
271	            raise ValueError("artifact plan must account for every requested kind")
272	        paths = [payload.target_leaf.casefold() for payload in self.payloads]
273	        if len(paths) != len(set(paths)):
274	            raise ValueError("planned target paths must be portable-case unique")
275	        return self

29	def validate_source_relative_path(value: str) -> str:
30	    """Return one normalized portable relative source path."""
31	
32	    if (
33	        not value
34	        or "\\" in value
35	        or value.startswith("/")

37	        or ":" in value

39	        raise ValueError("main_tex must be a normalized portable relative path")
40	    parts = value.split("/")
41	    if any(validate_deposit_slug(part) != part for part in parts):
42	        raise ValueError("main_tex must be a normalized portable relative path")
43	    if not value.casefold().endswith(".tex"):

45	    return value

47	
48	def _metadata_fallback_names(value: object) -> tuple[str, ...] | None:
49	    """Return distinct configured provider names without resolving them."""
50	
51	    if value is None:
52	        return None
53	    if isinstance(value, str) or not isinstance(value, (list, tuple)):
54	        raise ValueError("fallback_sources must be a sequence or null")
55	    result: list[str] = []
56	    seen: set[str] = set()
57	    for item in value:
58	        if not isinstance(item, str) or not item.strip():
59	            raise ValueError("metadata fallback names must be non-empty strings")
60	        name = item.strip()
61	        key = name.casefold()
62	        if key in seen:
63	            raise ValueError("metadata fallback names must be unique")
64	        seen.add(key)
65	        result.append(name)
66	    return tuple(result)
67

17	class ArticleCatalogSnapshot:
18	    """Direct-child article membership observed without publishing an inventory."""
19	
20	    name: str
21	    catalog_dir: str
22	    article_count: int
23	    slugs: tuple[str, ...]
24	
25	    def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, object]:
26	        return {
27	            "name": self.name,
28	            "catalog_dir": self.catalog_dir,
29	            "article_count": self.article_count,
30	            "slugs": list(self.slugs),
31	        }

33	
34	class ArticleCatalogService:
35	    """Delegate named catalog operations to jsonl_engine."""
36	
37	    def __init__(self, catalog_roots: ArticleCatalogRoots) -> None:
38	        self._catalog_roots = catalog_roots
39	
40	    def catalogs(self) -> tuple[ArticleCatalogDescriptor, ...]:
41	        """Return the configured catalog descriptors."""
42	
43	        return self._catalog_roots.catalogs()
44	
45	    def resolve(self, name: str) -> ArticleCatalogDescriptor:
46	        """Resolve one configured catalog name."""
47	
48	        return self._catalog_roots.resolve(name)
49	
50	    def inspect(self, name: str) -> ArticleCatalogSnapshot:
51	        """Validate path topology and report direct-child article membership."""
52	        descriptor = self.resolve(name)
53	        article_paths = discover_article_paths(
54	            descriptor.catalog_dir,
55	            publication_root=descriptor.publication_root,
56	        )
57	        slugs = tuple(os.path.basename(os.path.dirname(path)) for path in article_paths)
58	        return ArticleCatalogSnapshot(
59	            name=descriptor.name,
60	            catalog_dir=descriptor.catalog_dir,
61	            article_count=len(slugs),
62	            slugs=slugs,
63	        )
64	
65	    def rebuild(self, name: str, *, force: bool = False) -> InventoryCatalogResult:
66	        """Publish the named catalog inventory from safe direct-child discovery."""
67	        descriptor = self.resolve(name)
68	        return build_inventory(
69	            catalog_dir=descriptor.catalog_dir,
70	            force=force,
71	            publication_root=descriptor.publication_root,
72	        )
73	
74	
75	__all__ = ["ArticleCatalogService", "ArticleCatalogSnapshot"]
